# SF-H1: Where does legitimate use of another model's outputs end and unauthorised distillation, training one's own model on those outputs, begin?

Area H · As of clef 7924 Berichte · Luna 7924 Berichte · JEV 5667 Berichte · amos 7924 Berichte · SubQ 7924 Berichte · drex 7924 Berichte · d3-ultra 5449 Berichte · d3-flash 1675 Berichte

## Subquestions

- [SF-H1-T01](../../fragen/SF-H1/T01/): By what agreed criteria should governments distinguish legitimate research use of publicly available AI outputs from distillation that systematically undermines another nation's safety investments? Will they publish a shared, narrow definition of which model-output uses each of them considers a violation, and keep a shared, auditable register of verified unauthorised attempts or of licensed distillation? How otherwise does each government show that it is enforcing the agreement rather than using the accusation selectively?
- [SF-H1-T02](../../fragen/SF-H1/T02/): What evidence would a lab be willing to share with its own government when a foreign lab appears to have extracted capabilities from one of its models, and would that lab's government accept the same format in return?
- [SF-H1-T03](../../fragen/SF-H1/T03/): Which technical controls and audit trails let labs detect, report and prevent unauthorised distillation and the exfiltration of model weights (the learned parameters) across borders?
- [SF-H1-T04](../../fragen/SF-H1/T04/): Which transparency measures on distillation methods could labs adopt to address safety concerns without giving away proprietary innovation?
- [SF-H1-T05](../../fragen/SF-H1/T05/): How can states cooperate to monitor the proliferation of capabilities transferred via distillation from large, safety-aligned models into smaller, less governed ones?

## Reports by share of levels 4–7

Levels 4–7 / 3–7 = probability each model gives to these levels (3 = useful background or context, 4 = contains some facts that could be cited as evidence … 7 = directly and substantially answers the question). Listed: reports where clef or Luna give levels 3–7 more than 50 %. Sorted by the higher of clef and Luna for levels 4–7. Further models (JEV, amos, d3 …) rated part of the reports; the others show “not yet rated”. The full distribution over the 8 levels is in the data file.
